[2026-05-25 21:01:02] Generated response: 
 Courts can provide various remedies to affected communities, depending on the specific circumstances of the case. These remedies may include:

1. Injunctive relief: Courts can issue injunctions to prevent further harm to the environment or to require the responsible party to take specific actions to mitigate the damage.

2. Compensatory damages: Affected communities may be entitled to financial compensation for the harm they have suffered, including the cost of cleanup, medical expenses, and loss of property value.
